| **Conflict** | SS gates the crops in its season block tags at `BlockStateBase.randomTick` HEAD (`MixinBlockStateBase` -> `SeasonalCropGrowthHandler.onCropGrowth`, SS 9.1.0.3 SeasonalCropGrowthHandler.java:29-60), naming each plant by block or seed. BTW wheat (block_1089, top block_1090) and hemp (block_241) are ProxyBlocks in none of those tags, and tagging the blocks would cancel their whole FC tick, upkeep and the night reset of the grown-today flag included (Common FCBlockCropsDailyGrowth.java:41-49), so BTW crops ignored the seasons that vanilla-id plants follow (#016). FC's cold rules read FC's fixed 1.5.2 table (`FCUtilsMisc.IsIKInColdBiome`, temperature 0.15 or less) and 1.20.1's unseasonal `coldEnoughToSnow` (`WorldBridge.IsSnowingAtPos`), while SS's `Level.isRainingAt` hook already made BTW's rain seasonal (`IsRainingAtPos`, WorldBridge.java:2742-2744). In a temperate SS winter FC therefore melted placed ice at once, crumbled BTW solid snow to loose snow, and saw neither rain nor snow, so farmland never reverted. Bone meal: since #017 BTW's fertilizer runs in 1.20.1's use order, after Forge's `RightClickBlock` (ServerPlayerGameModeMixin.java:421-429), and SS's veto is a GlitchCore listener fed from that event at NORMAL (SS SeasonalCropGrowthHandler.java:62-121). Out of season it ate the bone meal (5 times in 6 under behaviour 0, always under 1) or broke the plant (behaviour 2) on SS-tagged FC-owned plants, where 4.B's rule is soil fertilizer |
| **Pattern** | **D** (FC-extend): SS's seasons act through BTW's own growth roll, snow test and cold table, by two provider seams that name no mod, and SS's own data names BTW's seeds and the lit light bulb. One `require = 0` mixin makes SS's bone-meal veto stand aside where BTW's bone-meal rule owns the block: no event lets BTW act before GlitchCore's NORMAL `RightClickBlock` listener without cancelling 1.20.1's use path (Protocol #4). Rebalance: none beyond SS's own rules |
| **Implementation** | [`SereneSeasonsCompat`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/sereneseasons/SereneSeasonsCompat.java)`.init`, called only from [`BTWCompat`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/BTWCompat.java) (:115-123, #020) inside its own try/catch: reflective probes that never initialize a class (fertility: the static `ModFertility.isCrop` and `isCropFertile` and `ModConfig.fertility` with `seasonalCrops` and `outOfSeasonCropBehavior`; climate: the static `SeasonHooks.coldEnoughToSnowSeasonal(LevelReader, Holder, BlockPos)`); each probe switches on only its own feature, and one INFO line reports both. [`SereneSeasonsSeasonGate`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/sereneseasons/SereneSeasonsSeasonGate.java), the `SeasonGate` provider, makes every SS decision, in order: ALLOW when `seasonalCrops` is off, when SS tags the state itself (`ModFertility.isCrop`), when the key is in none of the four season item or block tags, or when `ModFertility.isCropFertile` or greenhouse glass in the 16 cells above says so; otherwise behaviour 1 is DENY, 2 is DESTROY (DENY for an `#sereneseasons:unbreakable_infertile_crops` state), and 0 is ALLOW only on the cell's growing day, `(murmurHash3Mixer(getSeed(x, y, z) ^ floorDiv(dayTime + 2000, 24000)) & 0x7fffffff) % 6 == 0`. [`SeasonalCropGate`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/sereneseasons/SeasonalCropGate.java): `CropGrowEvent.Pre` at HIGH, posted at FC's growth roll by [`FcCropGrowthHooks`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/fcext/FcCropGrowthHooks.java) (#022); it acts only on a DEFAULT result, a server level and a ProxyBlock with a known seed, and turns DESTROY into DENY plus `destroyBlock(pos, false)` (FC's failed roll jumps straight to return). [`FcCropSeasonNames`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/sereneseasons/FcCropSeasonNames.java): a crop's key is its seed's, from FC's seed table reversed (the public int `m_iCropBlockID` of FCItemSeeds and FCItemSeedFood, ProxyBlock crops only), built once after FC init; the wheat top takes the crop below. [`SereneSeasonsClimate`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/sereneseasons/SereneSeasonsClimate.java): the `BTWClimate` provider, `SeasonHooks.coldEnoughToSnowSeasonal`. [`SereneSeasonsTagProvider`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/data/SereneSeasonsTagProvider.java) (runData, keys through `FcRefs`, #020): `#sereneseasons:summer_crops` BTW wheat and hemp seeds, `autumn_crops` wheat seeds, `spring_crops` hemp seeds, `#sereneseasons:greenhouse_glass` the lit bulb (block_223), and `data/betterthanwolves/compat_refs/sereneseasons.json`. [`SeasonalBonemealVetoMixin`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/mixin/compat/sereneseasons/SeasonalBonemealVetoMixin.java) (`@Pseudo`, `remap = false`, `require = 0`, HEAD of the static `SeasonalCropGrowthHandler.applyBonemeal(PlayerInteractEvent$UseBlock)`): it skips SS's veto when the stack is bone meal and `BoneMealPolicy.route(level, pos, state)` is not VANILLA; it is prepared only with SS installed (`CompatMixinPlugin`, #020). The C1 seams it fills: [`SeasonGate`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/climate/SeasonGate.java) and [`BTWClimate`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/climate/BTWClimate.java) (one provider slot each; without one, ALLOW, and 1.20.1's verdict with FC's table; a failing provider is dropped once with one WARN), [`WorldBridge`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/WorldBridge.java)`.IsSnowingAtPos` (:2747-2754), and the load-time [`FCUtilsMiscColdBiomeMixin`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/mixin/fcext/FCUtilsMiscColdBiomeMixin.java) on FC's `IsIKInColdBiome`, which takes the live verdict only where it differs from 1.20.1's at the column's motion-blocking height. Build: `compileOnly` SS 9.1.0.3 and GlitchCore 0.0.1.1 (Forge/build.gradle:1934-1935), never bundled (both All Rights Reserved), and the dev token `sereneseasons` (:1863). Hooks: `BRIDGE_SPEC.md`, Mod compatibility (BTWCompat), seams SeasonGate and BTWClimate |
| **Effect** | Without SS nothing changes: no provider is set and the shipped tags load as inert data. With SS 9.1 or later (`[BTW-Compat] Serene Seasons 9.1.0.3: BTW crop seasons on, BTW climate on`): BTW wheat is a summer and autumn crop and hemp a spring and summer one, in SS's seed table, which its tooltips render. At FC's growth roll, after FC's own light, weed and hydration conditions, a BTW crop SS calls out of season (no greenhouse glass above, outside SS's underground rule) follows `out_of_season_crop_behavior`: 0, SS's default, grows only on one FC growth day in six per cell; 1 never grows; 2 breaks without drops (an unbreakable-tagged state only waits). FC's one step per day, the night reset of the grown-today flag, upkeep, weeds and hydration are untouched. One gate per crop: a state SS tags itself (FC-owned vanilla-id and twin plants, HarvestCraft crops through ss-hc2crops, a BTW crop block a pack tags) is gated by SS's own veto before TickPolicy's dispatch (#016), and the BTW gate answers ALLOW for it; an unlisted plant stays season-blind. Climate: with SS's default adjustments (winter -0.8, early spring and late autumn -0.25, otherwise 0, on every biome that is not tropical or blacklisted and has a base temperature of 0.8 or less; SS SeasonHooks.java:134-144) a temperate biome is cold enough to snow in winter, so placed ice stays ice, BTW solid snow holds, and winter snow reverts unlit, exposed farmland to loose dirt under a snow layer by FC's own rule (Common FCBlockFarmland.java:194-212); block light 10 or more at the crop's cell keeps the field. Where SS leaves 1.20.1's verdict unchanged, FC's table still decides. Bone meal: on BTW wheat and hemp (never SS crops) and, out of season, on the SS-tagged plants BoneMealPolicy owns (carrots, potatoes, vanilla-id wheat, stems, cane, cactus, cocoa, saplings, the beetroot and sapling twins), bone meal is FC fertilizer as in season (#017); routes BoneMealPolicy leaves to 1.20.1 keep SS's veto. SS's calendar and season sensor craft at the BTW workbench (#025). Dynamic Trees asks the same provider at its sapling-to-tree step, keyed by the species seed (#027) |
| **Lore mapping** | BTW wheat is wheat: SS's own seasons for vanilla wheat (summer, autumn). Hemp is a warm-season fibre crop (spring, summer). The lit light bulb counts as greenhouse glass for the column below it, so a bulb-lit hemp room is a greenhouse; the unlit bulb is not |
| **BTW adds** | Seasons act through BTW's own rules: behaviour 0 thins FC growth days rather than rolls (BTW wheat rolls about 12 times a day and grows once, so SS's per-roll 5-in-6 veto would slow it only about 1.8x; decision #10); winter threatens fields through FC's snow reversion, so light becomes a winter tool; bulb-lit hemp rooms grow through winter; bone meal stays 4.B fertilizer in every season. The two seams belong to no mod, and Dynamic Trees' sapling step uses SeasonGate too |
| **Dual-sourced?** | Yes — SS's gate, bone-meal veto, tooltips and climate keep every block and route BTW does not own; BTW only adds tag entries (`required: false`, `replace: false`) and answers for its own ProxyBlock crops |
| **Data or code?** | Both: five generated files (four SS tags and the compat_refs manifest) and code (five compat classes, one data provider, one mixin into SS) on the C1 seams |
| **Alt rejected** | Putting BTW crop blocks in SS's block tags: SS's veto cancels the whole random tick, so out of season FC's upkeep, `UpdateIfBlockStays` and the night reset would stop too, and SS's per-tick 5-in-6 would slow daily wheat only about 1.8x. GlitchCore events or `sereneseasons.api` only: the public API exposes the season but not the fertility rules, the out-of-season behaviour or the seasonal temperature, so BTW would have to copy them, and SS is All Rights Reserved |
| **Alt rejected (2)** | A `hasPrecipitation()` guard in `IsSnowingAtPos` or in the provider (a C1 review finding): 1.20.1 lays snow wherever it rains and `Biome.shouldSnow` passes (ServerLevel.java:442-456, Biome.java:176-189), and neither that nor SS's snow hook asks about precipitation, so FC's "snowing" must match where snow layers form; a cold biome without precipitation is not among the vanilla overworld biomes SS adjusts. A seasonal temperature in `WorldBridge.getBiomeGenForCoords`: every FC biome read, rain, grass colour and spawning included, would follow the season. For bone meal: a HIGHEST `RightClickBlock` listener that runs BTW's path and cancels the event (it re-implements 1.20.1's use order, where 4.B activates the block, weed pull included, before the held item, and duplicates BoneMealPolicy), or accepting SS's veto (it eats the item or breaks the plant where 4.B's fertilizer rule applies) |
| **Known limitation** | SS's fertility and climate classes are internal (`sereneseasons.init`, `sereneseasons.season`, not `sereneseasons.api`). If a later SS changes them, the probes switch the feature off with one INFO line, SeasonGate and BTWClimate drop a throwing provider, and the crop gate switches itself off once (BTW crops season-blind until restart); the veto mixin silently does nothing, and H3 is its only runtime proof. Decision #8: listed BTW seeds follow SS's cold-position rule (SS ModFertility.java:99-108): where 1.20.1 calls a position too cold to rain, only winter crops are fertile, so exposed BTW wheat and hemp there never come into season. Behaviour 0 keys on `getDayTime()`: with `doDaylightCycle false` a cell's growing day never changes (SS's season clock, which follows day time, stands still too). The shipped data names this build's keys (item_22591, item_482, block_223): a moved BTW ID config makes them stale, the compat-refs audit WARNs once, and BTW wheat goes season-blind without an error (Follow-up from #005). A pack that adds a BTW crop block to SS's block tags hands it to SS's own veto, which out of season cancels the whole FC random tick, upkeep and the night reset included; BTW's data never does. SS's spring melt calls 1.20.1's `IceBlock.melt` (SS RandomUpdateHandler.java:86-91), which places a water source and bypasses FC's melt (non-source ice bit, Common FCBlockIce.java:163-172): right for the ponds SS froze, but ice a player placed during a temperate winter, which FC would have turned into non-persistent water outside a cold biome (FCBlockIce.java:83-89), becomes a source in spring. FC-rendered captures (a grass block lifted by a platform) draw FC's constant grass colour (Modern-Common BiomeGenBase.java:98-99), so they stay summer-green while SS recolours world grass and leaves (by code reading). Cold Sweat applies SS's seasons only for SS 9.1.0.0-9.3.0.26 (#030), so with 9.3.0.27 Cold Sweat loses its season modifier. The legacy 9.0.0.46 line, by javap: the fertility probe fails (no `ModFertility.isCrop`, ForgeConfigSpec configs), so the BTW crop gate stays off and the veto mixin has no target; the climate provider works; legacy SS's own `CropGrowEvent` listener gates every crop by block name, so BTW wheat and hemp, unlisted there, are season-blind where it rains and never in season at cold positions (decision #19's "only if a pack tags them" does not hold) |
| **Verified by** | Dev-server proofs with `-PbtwDevMods=sereneseasons`, SS 9.1.0.3 (C2 after-runs, 2026-10-17/18). On HEAD 3985147 (`ss_baseline.txt`) the 17 gap checks fail (A1, A2, A4, A5, B2-B4, C1, D1, F1-F3, G1, G4, G5, H3, M1) and the 20 controls pass, among them I1/I2: SS vetoes FC carrots and the beetroot twin before TickPolicy's dispatch, so #016's ordering holds at runtime (mixin order BlockStateBaseTickMixin, SS `MixinBlockStateBase`, BlockStateRandomTickMixin). After C2, `ss_after.txt` 51/51: SS's seed table gives BTW wheat seeds mask 6 (summer, autumn) and hemp seeds 3 (spring, summer); in early_winter, behaviour 1 keeps 16/16 wheat and 16/16 hemp at level 0, behaviour 0 leaves 136 of 160 wheat at level 0 on one growth day (window 118-148; 0 at HEAD), behaviour 2 destroys 16/16 with no drops; mid_summer growth and the night reset are unchanged; in mid_winter plains placed ice stays, BTW solid snow (block_1049) holds, 16/16 unlit farmland reverts and 16/16 lit farmland stays, and mid_summer ice melts; the provider's verdicts G1-G7; bone meal H1-H3 (an out-of-season FC carrot's soil fertilized, one bone meal used); I1-I3; exposed snowy-plains wheat 16/16 at level 0 in mid_summer, grown under a glass roof (M1, M2); a glass roof (K1); bulb-lit hemp grows in early_winter while sunlit hemp without a bulb stays at level 0 (L1, L2); an earlier ALLOW wins (N1); one gate for a pack-tagged block_1089 (O1); SS's 2 recipes craft at the BTW workbench (J1). The first after-run's I1/I2 failures were plan noise: leftover rain fell as snow in SS's winter and reverted those rows by the new farmland rule; the plan now waits out the rain fade. Logs: `[CLIMATE] SeasonGate provider set: SereneSeasonsSeasonGate`, `[CLIMATE] BTWClimate provider set: SereneSeasonsClimate`, `[BTW-Compat] sereneseasons 9.1.0.3 present: integration #028 enabled`, `[BTW-Compat] Serene Seasons: BTW crop seeds 1089 -> betterthanwolves:item_22591, 241 -> betterthanwolves:item_482`, `Mixing compat.sereneseasons.SeasonalBonemealVetoMixin`. SS's own `fertility.toml` with behaviour 1, 3/3; plain BTW 6/6 (BTW crops grow, plains ice melts, the shipped tags load inert, `/reload` is clean, no SS class in the log); a moved wheat-seed id 4/4 (`1 stale (1 warned, 0 inert)`, wheat season-blind, hemp still gated); with HarvestCraft and ss-hc2crops 7/7 (SS gates HC corn, the BTW gate BTW wheat, both grow in mid_summer); the all-mods server 10/10 (E1: bone meal on an out-of-season FC carrot fertilizes its soil); a dev client join 1/1, `mixerr: 0`. C1 seam proofs with test providers and no season mod: climate 7/7, SeasonGate 5/5. Production smoke (2026-10-18): the reobfuscated `BetterThanWolves-1.20.1-1.6.2-alpha.5.jar` on a Forge 47.3.0 dedicated server with SS 9.1.0.3, GlitchCore 0.0.1.1 and the other optional mods: the mixin applies, both providers are set, `#028 enabled`, 0 mixin errors, 20 TPS. Not runtime-tested: SS 9.3.0.27 (javap only), the legacy 9.0.0.46 line, the seed tooltip on screen, the client bone-meal swing with SS, Dynamic Trees seeds in SS's crop tags |
| **Net effect on FC loop** | Temperate winters become a farming threat through BTW's own rules: unlit, exposed fields revert under snow and out-of-season BTW crops stall; light, glass roofs and bulb-lit rooms answer it. In snowy biomes and high up, BTW wheat and hemp need greenhouse glass all year (under behaviour 0 they grow on one day in six without it); BTW wheat already needs natural light (Common FCBlockCropsDailyGrowth.java:54), which a glass roof keeps. Bone meal stays 4.B fertilizer in every season |

### #029 — AppleSkin

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Modern feature** | AppleSkin `appleskin` 2.5.1+mc1.20.1, a client-side mod: food values in item tooltips, the held-food preview on the food bar (hunger and saturation), the exhaustion underlay behind the food bar, the estimated-health hint on the heart row, a food line in F3, its on/off settings, and the `squeek.appleskin.api` events other mods listen to (`FoodValuesEvent`, `TooltipOverlayEvent`, `HUDOverlayEvent`) |
| **Added** | Third-party mod. Its 1.20.1-forge line is final (last commit 2023-09-27). BTW accepts 2.5.0 and later (`[2.5.0,)`, side CLIENT, mods.toml:69-77); 2.5.0 has the same API |
| **Conflict** | AppleSkin models vanilla's 0-20 hunger and saturation; BTW feeds FC's 60-point hunger with overflow-only fat, penalty levels and food poisoning (#021, #024). At 3985147 every AppleSkin display was wrong, missing or invisible. Tooltips gave FC-rebalanced foods their vanilla values (bread: 5 hunger and 0.6 saturation, 2.5 shanks, where BTW feeds 9/60, 1.5 shanks) and showed raw beef as safe (FC poisons it 30% of the time). The milk bucket and pumpkin seeds, which BTW feeds but which have no 1.20.1 `FoodProperties`, had no food tooltip at all (AppleSkin's `isFood`, FoodHelper.java:17-20). The HUD preview and the exhaustion underlay hang off `food_level`, which BTW cancels at HIGHEST to draw its own row (BTWHudOverlay.java:72-79, #023), so they never drew. The health hint predicted vanilla saturation regeneration, which BTW does not have (FC heals above 24/60, #021), and drew at y = screen height, off-screen, because its row offset is captured only in `food_level` (HUDOverlayHandler.java:96, 132). F3 read vanilla's FoodData copy: `hunger: 13, sat: 0, exh: 0.00/4` at FC 40/60 with FC exhaustion 0.665 |
| **Pattern** | **D** (FC-extend): every AppleSkin display is rebuilt on AppleSkin's own API events and on BTW's food-row extension (#023), and shows BTW's model. Nothing is subtracted (Protocol #8): AppleSkin's tooltip slot (for foods FC values) and its four `HUDOverlayEvent` draws are cancelled, and BTW redraws each one with BTW's numbers, under AppleSkin's own settings. Layering by compat decision 17 (2026-10-11): BTW shows its food model through AppleSkin only, plus the regen-resumes marker on the heart row |
| **Implementation** | [`AppleSkinCompat`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/appleskin/AppleSkinCompat.java)`.initClient`, reached only from [`BTWCompat`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/BTWCompat.java) (:124-132) by a direct `invokestatic`, on a client with AppleSkin loaded. It first links every AppleSkin member it uses (preflight), then registers: `FoodValuesEvent` (FC values in AppleSkin's units), `TooltipOverlayEvent.Pre` (cancelled, and the stack marked, for every food FC values), `RenderTooltipEvent.GatherComponents` at HIGHEST (clears the mark) and LOW (adds BTW's tooltip), the four `HUDOverlayEvent`s (cancelled), `CustomizeGuiOverlayEvent.DebugText` (F3), a client tick (AppleSkin's flash cycle) and `RenderGuiOverlayEvent` Pre and Post on `player_health` (the heart hint). [`BtwFoodTooltip`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/appleskin/BtwFoodTooltip.java) and [`BtwFoodTooltipRenderer`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/appleskin/BtwFoodTooltipRenderer.java) (the tooltip component and its factory). [`AppleSkinHud`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/appleskin/AppleSkinHud.java), a [`FoodHudExtension`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/client/FoodHudExtension.java) registered at client setup (the underlay under BTW's pips, the previews over them through `BTWHudOverlay.renderer()`), with the eat gates and the heart hint. [`AppleSkinClientOptions`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/appleskin/AppleSkinClientOptions.java) reads AppleSkin's CLIENT config by key through fmlcore's `ConfigTracker`, so no AppleSkin class is named. [`AppleSkinProbe`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/appleskin/AppleSkinProbe.java), a dev diagnostic, inert unless `-Dbtw.hudProbe` or `BTW_HUD_PROBE` is set. Values come from [`FcFoodValues`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/food/FcFoodValues.java)`.of` / `preview` (#024) and from the client's FC food, fat and exhaustion in `PenaltySync` ([`BTWNetwork`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/BTWNetwork.java), protocol 5, #021). Build: `compileOnly` of AppleSkin's api jar (Forge/build.gradle:1936) and the `appleskin` dev token (:1865); text from the eight `btw.compat.food.*` lang keys (en_us.json:82-89). Hooks: `BRIDGE_SPEC.md`, Mod compatibility (BTWCompat), seam FoodHudExtension |
| **Effect** | Tooltips: for every food FC values, BTW's tooltip takes AppleSkin's place. It shows FC shanks at 1/6-shank resolution (bread 1.5 shanks, a melon slice a third of one), green shanks plus a first row for harmful effects ("30% food poisoning" on raw beef, "80% food poisoning" on rotten flesh, "Poison" on spider eyes), and a second row for what eating does now: "+0.42 fat" (cooked beef at 50/60), "+5 fat, -> Chubby" (pumpkin pie at 60/60 with 10 fat), "clears Peckish, regen resumes" (bread at 20/60), or "(full)" where FC refuses the food (cooked beef at 60/60). The tooltip takes one of two paths. Marked: AppleSkin's own show rule passed and BTW cancelled its `TooltipOverlayEvent.Pre`, so BTW's component goes in its place (a copied stack still matches through `ItemStack.isSameItemSameTags`). Unmarked: a stack FC feeds that AppleSkin cannot see (no `FoodProperties`; with AppleSkin alone, exactly the milk bucket and pumpkin seeds) gets BTW's tooltip under AppleSkin's own rule, with Shift while `showFoodValuesInTooltip` is on or always while `showFoodValuesInTooltipAlways` is on (TooltipOverlayHandler.java:322). HUD: the held food's preview flashes on BTW's pips at AppleSkin's alpha, food at 1/6-shank resolution plus the fat an overflow adds. It shows only for food whose eat would start: from the main hand when FC's eat or vanilla's would start it, from the off hand (while AppleSkin's off-hand setting is on) only when the 1.20.1 item's own `use` would, never for a BTW item there, and never under Hunger. It also shows while mounted, because BTW draws its food row there (BTWHudOverlay.java:93). The underlay fills AppleSkin's strip with FC exhaustion against FC's burn threshold: 1.33 for hunger, or 0.5, tinted orange, while fat burns first; costs from other mods reach FC exhaustion (#021), so they show too. The heart hint flashes the health that eating the held food would restore: 1 HP when the food lifts FC food from 24/60 or below to above it (BTW's regeneration resumes), plus the healing ticks of a guaranteed Regeneration effect. AppleSkin's own hide rules apply (hurt, not peaceful, no Poison, Wither or Regeneration, no hidden GUI, survival only); its vanilla food-below-18 test becomes FC's 24/60 hinge. With Legendary Survival Overhaul the hint stays on the vanilla heart row, under LSO's health overlay. F3: AppleSkin's line is replaced in place by `BTW food: 40/60, fat: 0, exh: 0.67/1.33 (hunger)`. Each of AppleSkin's settings switches its BTW counterpart. Third-party AppleSkin listeners receive FC values in AppleSkin's units (bread: hunger 3, default saturation modifier 0.125) |
| **BTW adds** | BTW's food economy becomes readable in game: 60-point values, overflow-only fat, penalty steps, poisoning odds, FC exhaustion and the real regeneration hinge, which 4.B left to the wiki (`docs/curseforge-feedback-triage.md`, CTX-3). Foods from other mods that BTW values (#024) show their BTW values the same way |
| **Dual-sourced?** | Yes — AppleSkin keeps every feature, setting and API event, and other mods' listeners still receive every event. Without AppleSkin no class of the integration loads and the HUD is unchanged; on a dedicated server BTW loads nothing for AppleSkin, whose own server half is untouched. If any handler throws, the whole integration switches off with one ERROR and AppleSkin's own displays return |
| **Data or code?** | Code: six client classes behind one `BTWCompat` dispatch block; no mixin and no data. Only `AppleSkinCompat` names an AppleSkin type, all from `squeek.appleskin.api` |
| **Alt rejected** | Mixins into AppleSkin's `FoodHelper` and HUD handler: its internals are not API, and its events reach every display. Answering only through `FoodValuesEvent`: its integer hunger cannot express 1/6 shanks, overflow-only fat or poisoning, and AppleSkin's own drawing would still model vanilla hunger |
| **Alt rejected (2)** | Answering AppleSkin's `HUDOverlayEvent`s instead of drawing on BTW's row: three of the four never fire under BTW's row, and their values are vanilla 0-20 numbers. Skipping the heart hint while Legendary Survival Overhaul is loaded (the first plan): LSO 2.4.7 registers its `health_overhaul` overlay above `player_health` and never cancels vanilla's heart row (checked with javap), so the hint stays correct under LSO's overlay. Drawing the tooltip shanks with `FCHudRendererBridge.drawFoodPreview`: it shifts each pip by the HUD's last hunger shake |
| **Known limitation** | The client cannot read the `naturalRegeneration` gamerule (the server does not sync it), so the heart hint assumes it is on; with the rule off, BTW does not heal (#021), but the hint still shows the regeneration half heart. With LSO, the 24/60 hinge the hint marks holds through #031's regeneration gate. Third-party AppleSkin listeners see `ceil(hunger60 / 3)` and a fat-based saturation modifier, AppleSkin's API being in vanilla integer units. The tooltip relies on AppleSkin posting `TooltipOverlayEvent.Pre` from its own `GatherComponents` listener with the hovered stack (TooltipOverlayHandler.java:307-310); a miss shows no BTW tooltip, never wrong numbers. The eat gates copy BTW's two eat paths, so a later change to either could show a preview for food that cannot be eaten (display only). The heart hint copies vanilla's heart layout and low-health jitter; a mod that moves vanilla's heart row would misalign it (LSO 2.4.7 and Cold Sweat do not). Client values arrive with `PenaltySync` and can be one tick old. Numbers use the default locale's decimal separator, as AppleSkin's own do, and the penalty names are FC's English names |
| **Verified by** | javap: `BTWCompat` has no `invokedynamic` and calls `AppleSkinCompat.initClient` by `invokestatic`; `squeek/` appears only in `AppleSkinCompat`, and the release jar holds no `squeek/` class. Dev-server proof (AppleSkin 2.5.1, dedicated server), 15/15 checks: `btwproof foodgap` lists exactly the milk bucket (h60 6) and pumpkin seeds (h60 1); 13 eat predictions match the real eat (bread at 40 to 49; bread at 20 resumes regeneration; cooked beef at 50 gains 0.42 fat; cooked beef at 60 is refused; a cookie at 60 gains 1 fat; pumpkin pie at 60 with 10 fat gains 5 and reaches fat penalty 2; melon, pumpkin seeds, milk at 60, chocolate milk, the donut, cooked mutton from the `btw_foods` table, the golden apple); the server log has no line or class load of the integration. Dev-client proofs (2026-10-17, GUI scale 2), every check passed and every logged state matched: the tooltips above on both paths, under AppleSkin's show rule; the HUD preview per hand and gate (main-hand cookie at 60/60 previews fat only; off-hand cookie at 60/60, off-hand donut and anything under Hunger preview nothing; the preview while mounted); the underlay at 40/81 for 0.665/1.33 and for 0.25/0.5 while burning fat; the heart hint at 10 HP (bread at 20/60: 10 to 11; golden apple: 10 to 12; enchanted golden apple: 10 to 20, capped), with AppleSkin's own estimate cancelled when it still posts one (`modifiedHealth=14.0 canceled=true`); the F3 line; each setting tested (all but `showVanillaAnimationsOverlay`) hides its BTW counterpart; BTW's HUD layout is unchanged with the extension (`ext=1`). With LSO, the hint draws on the vanilla heart row (the screenshot shows the half heart in the sixth slot) and the preview stays on BTW's frost-tinted pips (#031). Throw mode: exactly one ERROR, then AppleSkin's own tooltip returns (`AS(def=5/0.6 mod=5/0.6)`). Without AppleSkin the client log equals the pre-C2 reference. Production (2026-10-18): a Forge 47.3.0 dedicated server with the release jar, AppleSkin 2.5.1 and the five other mods boots clean. Not run: a production client (the Modrinth 2.5.1 jar, then 2.5.0, then none); 2.5.0 is accepted on API equality. Not runtime-tested: HarvestCraft meals in the tooltip, GUI scales other than 2 |
| **Net effect on FC loop** | None: display only. FC eating, food values and the HUD layout are unchanged |

### #030 — Cold Sweat

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Modern feature** | Cold Sweat `cold_sweat` 2.4.3.2 (2.4.1 also accepted): body temperature from the world and from nearby heat sources, worn insulation (armour, and items sewn into armour), warming foods, fuelled devices (hearth, boiler), and freeze effects that run between body -50 and -100 (slower healing, mining and movement, cold damage) |
| **Added** | Third-party mod for Forge 1.20.1. BTW builds against 2.4.3.2 (CurseForge file 8907301, `compileOnly`) and accepts `[2.4.1,)` (optional, ordered after Cold Sweat, mods.toml:79-86) |
| **Decisions** | Compat decisions 1, 14 and 15 (2026-10-11; the code cites 14 and 15 by number): with LSO also installed, BTW feeds both mods and logs one WARN (1); the values are anchored on Cold Sweat's own (14: the large campfire is its fire and campfire, lava cracks its magma block, gimp its leather); BTW fat matters in the cold (15: here freezing burns it; under LSO, #031, it insulates). Settled 2026-10-17 under the maintainer's rulings (post-1.5.2 content takes its nearest FC relative's rule; integrate, don't disable): lit candles, candle cakes and the lantern warm like BTW's candle and torch; waterskins get no BTW campfire-spit path, Cold Sweat's own boiler heats them. Decided 2026-10-17/18: shivering costs no FC hunger under Cold Sweat's Grace effect or on peaceful |
| **Conflict** | Cold Sweat knows only vanilla content. At 3985147, with both mods installed, its registry load received no BTW entry (`Loaded 0 insulators`, `0 fuels`, `0 foods`, `0 block temperatures`): every BTW heat source, piece of clothing, cauldron meal and fuel was invisible to it, and stoking a fire lowered warmth (`minecraft:fire` 25 F, the stoked fire 251 it becomes 0 F). FC content on vanilla ids got nothing either: the legacy torch (FC id 50, which 1.20.1 world generation places) and mushroom stew, which 4.B cooks in the cauldron. Freezing cost no hunger, so BTW's fat and food played no part in the cold. Static Cold Sweat data would not hold: BTW's registry names (`block_<id>`, `item_<id>`) move with the BTW ID config (Follow-up from #005) |
| **Pattern** | **D** (FC-extend): BTW's climate content becomes Cold Sweat data, and Cold Sweat's freezing feeds FC's hunger rule. Nothing of Cold Sweat is disabled; rebalance: none (the values are Cold Sweat's own anchors) |
| **Implementation** | [`ColdSweatCompat`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/coldsweat/ColdSweatCompat.java)`.init`, reached only from [`BTWCompat`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/BTWCompat.java) (:133-141, #020) when Cold Sweat is loaded: it probes Cold Sweat's types, then adds two Forge-bus listeners, so an incompatible version fails before anything is registered. On Cold Sweat's `LoadRegistriesEvent.Pre` (each server start, after FC init, and each `/coldsweat reload`) it builds block temperatures, insulators, foods and fuels from [`BtwThermalTable`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/thermal/BtwThermalTable.java) (#020) with live keys from [`FcRefs`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/FcRefs.java), decodes each with Cold Sweat's own codec, rejects any decode error (partial results included), re-checks the fields 1.20.1's lenient decoder would silently default (insulation cold and heat; block range, max effect and units; fuel value), and adds the accepted entries together at the end. Heat rows merge per block and tier into one entry with a `meta` state (a `{min,max}` range or a list of values), so the hibachi's two ranges and each loose block's eight odd metas are one entry each; a tier without a value is skipped with one WARN, never guessed. [`ColdSweatJson`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/coldsweat/ColdSweatJson.java) writes Cold Sweat's JSON shapes with Gson only. [`ColdSweatShivering`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/coldsweat/ColdSweatShivering.java) is the server `PlayerTickEvent` END listener. `BtwThermalTable` gained `HeatTier.LAVA_CRACKS` (16 single-meta rows, BtwThermalTable.java:91-94), `vanillaHeatSources()` (5 rows, :138-143) and `vanillaHotMeals()` (mushroom stew, :145-146), which #031 reads too. Isolation: `BTWCompat` catches `Throwable` around `init`; the registry and tick handlers each catch `RuntimeException \| LinkageError`, log one ERROR, switch themselves off and never throw into Cold Sweat. No mixin, no shipped Cold Sweat data, no Cold Sweat class in the jar. Table contract: `BRIDGE_SPEC.md`, Mod compatibility (BTWCompat), BtwThermalTable |
| **Effect** | Block temperatures (temperature / range / max effect, in F): embers (unlit campfire, meta 8-11) 6 / 3 / 12; small campfire 12 / 5 / 24; medium 20 / 6 / 40; large 25 / 7 / 50, Cold Sweat's own fire and campfire; stoked fire 35 / 7 / 70; lit hibachi (meta 4-7, 12-15) 6 / 2 / 12; burning brick oven 11.25 / 5 / 29.7; BTW's finite and nether-coal torches 2 / 2 / 4; BTW's candle 1 / 2 / 2; smouldering log 6 / 3 / 12; burning ladder 25 / 7 / 50; loose cobblestone and loose stone brick with lava in their cracks (odd meta) 20 / 3 / 48, Cold Sweat's own magma block. By vanilla key: the legacy torch (`minecraft:torch`, `minecraft:wall_torch`) and `minecraft:lantern` take the torch value; lit `#minecraft:candles` and `#minecraft:candle_cakes` take the candle value; unlit candles get nothing, and soul torches and soul lanterns are not listed. Worn insulation (cold/heat; head, chest, legs, feet): knit wool 6/0, 8/0, 7/0, 6/0 (the cold set); padded 7/4, 10/5, 9/5, 7/4 (balanced); tanned 6/6, 8/8, 7/7, 6/6 (leather plus); gimp 5/5, 7/7, 6/6, 5/5 (Cold Sweat's leather); plate none. Sewn into armour: wool 1/0, knit wool 1.5/0, padding 1.5/0.5, tanned leather 1.25/1.25. Meals: hearty stew +20 for 2400 ticks; chicken soup, fish soup and mushroom stew +15 for 1800 ticks. Fuels: every BTW item that burns in FC gives round(55 x FC burn time / 1600) units, Cold Sweat's coal being 55, in both the hearth and the boiler (39 items at the default ids; nethercoal 110, twice coal; coal dust 28); an item worth less than one unit is skipped. Shivering: once a second, while Cold Sweat's body temperature is below -50, the player pays 0.05 x factor FC exhaustion, the factor rising linearly from 0 at -50 to 1 at -100 (Cold Sweat's default freeze band). It goes through `causeFoodExhaustion`, so FC's rule applies (#021: armour weight, invulnerable exemption) and FC's FoodStats burns fat first while fat > ((food + 5) / 6) x 2 (Modern-Common FoodStats.java:214-216). Full freeze costs 1.5 per 600 ticks, three times FC's own time drain (0.5 per 600 ticks, vanilla/server EntityPlayerMP.java:992-993). Nothing is charged while the player is dead, in creative or spectator, under Cold Sweat's Grace effect (`cold_sweat:grace`, a new player's first 6000 ticks by default) or on peaceful, mirroring Cold Sweat's own immunity test, under which its freeze effects are off too. There is no heat counterpart. Cold Sweat's freeze slowdown reaches FC digging through the BreakSpeed post (#022), and its frozen-hearts cap clamps BTW's 600-tick heal, which goes through `LivingEntity.heal` (PlayerBridge.java:580-584) |
| **Lore mapping** | The fire ladder is BTW's cooking heat, so warmth rises embers < small < medium < large campfire < stoked fire, with the large campfire level with Cold Sweat's own fire. Loose cobblestone and loose stone brick are warm while lava sits in their cracks by FC's own rule (`FCBlockLavaReceiver.GetIsBlockWarm`, metadata bit 0, Server FCBlockLavaReceiver.java:99-109), so they take Cold Sweat's magma value. The legacy torch is a burning FC torch (`FCBlockTorchLegacy` extends `FCBlockTorchBaseBurning`, like the finite and nether-coal torches). A lit vanilla candle is BTW's candle, a candle cake carries a lit candle, and a lantern is a burning torch in a case. Mushroom stew is a 4.B cauldron soup: FC removes its crafting recipe and cooks it from 3 brown mushrooms, a milk bucket and 2 bowls (Server FCRecipes.java:3664-3673, :5167). Gimp is leather; knit wool keeps out cold only; padding adds a little heat protection; tanned leather is better leather |
| **BTW adds** | BTW's cooking-heat ladder is the warmth ladder, so tending and stoking a fire matters against the cold. The wool, padding and tanning chains gain a survival purpose: BTW clothing insulates, and BTW textiles are Cold Sweat sewing material. Cauldron meals warm over time. FC fuels, nethercoal at twice coal, run Cold Sweat's hearth and boiler. BTW fat is the body's fuel against cold: freezing burns it before hunger |
| **Dual-sourced?** | Yes — Cold Sweat's items, recipes, config and data defaults (fire, campfires, magma, leather, wool, soul sprout, coal fuels), its Serene Seasons compat and its freeze effects are untouched; BTW only adds entries. Its recipes craft at the BTW workbench through #025. Cold Sweat's own food-regeneration limit, which hooks `FoodData.tick`, never runs, because BTW cancels that tick at HEAD (FoodDataMixin.java:64-84); its frozen-hearts cap still applies through `LivingHealEvent` |
| **Data or code?** | Code that writes data: runtime registry entries through Cold Sweat's own event and codecs. No mixin and no shipped Cold Sweat data files |
| **Alt rejected** | Shipped Cold Sweat data keyed to `block_<id>` / `item_<id>`: the keys hold only for the default BTW ID config, while the runtime keys follow moved ids (P3 below). Cold Sweat's `BlockTemp` / `SimpleBlockTemp` classes: they break binary compatibility between 2.4.1 and 2.4.3. Adding BTW fires to `#minecraft:campfires` or `#minecraft:fire`: Cold Sweat's campfire entry needs `lit=true`, and the tags would pull in 1.20.1 fire behaviour |
| **Alt rejected (2)** | A waterskin path on BTW's campfire spit (port-side mixins on `FCBlockCampfire.IsValidCookItem` and `FCTileEntityCampfire.UpdateCookState`): it would bend two 4.B spit rules (the spit takes only FC campfire recipes, and anything left on a large fire turns to burned meat, Server FCTileEntityCampfire.java:438-449) and write Cold Sweat's internal `Temperature` NBT key, which is not API; kept as a follow-up if players ask. Leaving candles, candle cakes and the lantern cold, as Cold Sweat does: a lit vanilla candle is the same flame as BTW's candle. Shivering that ignores Grace: a new player would pay FC hunger for 6000 ticks while Cold Sweat's own freeze effects are off (a 3985147 client run frozen inside Grace healed from 14 to 16 and dug at the warm speed) |
| **Known limitation** | Cold Sweat and LSO (#031) together count BTW heat twice, and fat helps twice (shivering burns it, LSO counts it as insulation); `BTWCompat` logs one WARN (:96-103) recommending that Cold Sweat own temperature, with LSO's `"Temperature Enabled" = false` under `[core]` in `legendarysurvivaloverhaul-common.toml`. Cold Sweat 2.4.3.2 enables its own Serene Seasons compat only for SS 9.1.0.0 to 9.3.0.26, so with SS 9.3.0.27 Cold Sweat loses its seasonal temperature; BTW adds no season code for Cold Sweat. Cold Sweat's cold and heat damage detonates carried blasting oil, by BTW's any-damage rule (PlayerMixin `hurt` RETURN, PlayerMixin.java:401-415). The shiver band copies Cold Sweat's default `temp_effects` data (-50 / -100): a pack that edits it moves Cold Sweat's effects, not BTW's band. The immunity test reads Grace by registry id and peaceful from the difficulty, not from Cold Sweat's own peaceful-mode setting (FC charges no hunger on peaceful anyway, Modern-Common FoodStats.java:81-83); an immunity source a later Cold Sweat adds needs the test updated. BTW's entries are direct holders, so Cold Sweat's `Loaded N ...` lines print their names empty, and a pack's Cold Sweat registry modifier reaches them only through `matches`, not by id. All five vanilla-key rows are written without checking Cold Sweat's own data (2.4.3.2 has none for them). Fuel values are taken at damage 0. After a failed registry load, BTW's Cold Sweat entries stay off until the game or server restarts (one ERROR). 4.B has no recipe for wool boots (Server FCRecipes.java:910-931 make the helm, chest and leggings only), so their insulator matters only for boots obtained another way (creative, commands). Waterskins do not warm on BTW's spit: use Cold Sweat's boiler, which burns BTW fuels, or a vanilla campfire (#025). HarvestCraft soups and stews are not Cold Sweat meals yet (follow-up, once a verified HarvestCraft tag exists). Not runtime-tested: Cold Sweat 2.4.1 (the public signatures of the 12 Cold Sweat API classes used are identical to 2.4.3.2, checked with javap); the insulation tooltip on a client; shivering under Grace or on peaceful; the shiver cost in AppleSkin's exhaustion underlay (#029); blasting oil under Cold Sweat damage; a production client |
| **Verified by** | Logs: `[BTW-Compat] cold_sweat 2.4.3.2 present: integration #030 enabled`; `[BTW-ColdSweat] added 19 block temps, 20 insulators (16 armour, 4 textiles), 4 foods, 78 fuels (39 items x hearth+boiler, 0 below 1 unit skipped); 0 rejected`; then Cold Sweat's own `Loaded 20 insulators`, `Loaded 78 fuels`, `Loaded 4 foods`, `Loaded 19 block temperatures` (0 each at 3985147); `[SELF-AUDIT/thermal] 29 heat-source rows (14 blocks), 20 clothing, 4 textiles, 3 meals, 39 fuels (nethercoal betterthanwolves:item_481 = 3200); 0 warning(s)` (13 rows, 12 blocks before). Dev-server proofs, 2026-10-17, with 3985147 as the baseline: P1 content 55/55 (40 failed at 3985147): every tier at its value and every unlit, idle or lava-free state at 0, the stoked fire 35 F against fire's 25 F, the legacy torch and wall torch 2, lit plain and dyed candles and the lit candle cake 1, the lantern 2, the armour and textile values, the three soups and mushroom stew, nethercoal 110 and coal dust 28 in hearth and boiler, and Cold Sweat's own fire, campfire, leather chestplate, soul sprout and coal unchanged; Cold Sweat's recipes 27/27 craftable at the BTW workbench (#025). P2 shivering 13/13 (6 failed): body -100 for 200 shiver seconds charged 10 FC exhaustion and no vanilla exhaustion, then FC's food tick burned fat 20 -> 17.5 with food staying 30; body -75 charged 2.5 over 100 seconds; body -50, 0 and +100 and an invulnerable player charged nothing; hearty stew and mushroom stew add Cold Sweat's food modifier, bread does not. P3, moved ids on a fresh world: the large campfire as `block_3000` still 25 F, the knit-wool helm as `item_29256` still 6/0. P4 without Cold Sweat: boots, no `[BTW-ColdSweat]` line and no class-loading error; the release jar holds no `com/momosoftworks` class. P5 with Serene Seasons 9.1.0.3 and P6 with LSO 2.4.7: the entries still apply, and exactly one WARN with LSO. Client run (a real player, Grace cleared, body held at -95): food 60 -> 57 and 3.81 FC exhaustion over a 1280-tick frozen window (59 and 1.00 at 3985147, FC's own time drain only); health held at 14 under the frozen-hearts cap of 11; FC stone dig progress 0.00121 frozen against 0.00222 warm; the screenshots show Cold Sweat's gauges clear of BTW's pips and text; no mixin errors. Production smoke (2026-10-18, release jar on a Forge 47.3.0 dedicated server, Cold Sweat 2.4.3.2 beside the other five mods): the same `added` and `Loaded` lines, no BTW stack frame |
| **Net effect on FC loop** | Nights, rain, snowy biomes and Serene Seasons winters make campfire tending, wool and cooked meals matter beyond hunger. A lit campfire answers both gloom and cold. Freezing costs BTW fat and hunger by FC's fat-first rule, so a cold player eats more |

### #031 — Legendary Survival Overhaul

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Modern feature** | Legendary Survival Overhaul `legendarysurvivaloverhaul` 1.20.1-2.4.7, optional (`mods.toml` accepts `[1.20.1-2.4.7,)`, the one build inspected): body temperature from nearby blocks, worn and held items, foods and heater fuel; thirst; localized limb damage; a custom health regeneration that replaces natural regeneration under its default config; Cold Hunger, with its own food-bar overlay |
| **Added** | Third-party mod (Modrinth release 2026-08-09) |
| **Decisions** | Compat-wave decisions #1 (Cold Sweat and LSO are both fed; one WARN), #2 (the regeneration gate mixin ships), #14 (values anchored on LSO's own vanilla files), #15 (fat insulates), #16 (static data plus a start-up audit) and #18 (compile-only; nothing of LSO is bundled). C2 lso D1 (FC's lava-cracked loose stone is warm) and D2 (bone meal on LSO's fern crops on FC ground follows 4.B) are settled by the maintainer's rulings: bone meal = 4.B, and general rules over per-mod lists. Candles, candle cakes and the lantern take their nearest FC relative's tier, the lore rule of #018 |
| **Conflict** | C1 already routed LSO's food costs into FC hunger, made BTW's heal obey `naturalRegeneration`, refused drinks while food-poisoned (#021), posted `BreakEvent` for FC harvests (#022) and stacked LSO's thirst bar above BTW's pips (#023). What remained, measured on HEAD 3985147 (`lso_p0`, 19 gap checks): (1) under its default config LSO writes `naturalRegeneration=false` at level load, which stops BTW's heal (#021), and its custom regeneration heals whenever vanilla food is above 0, which is FC food 3/60 and up, while BTW heals only above 24/60 (Modern-Common FoodStats.java:87-94): hp 10 -> 11 at FC food 24 and at 3. (2) BTW's fires, clothing, soups, drinks and fuels were invisible to LSO: beside a BTW large campfire the target temperature read 11.2, the same as with no fire (a vanilla campfire reads 20.3); wool gave 0 cold resistance; plate gave 0 heat resistance and no limb armour; hearty stew, mushroom stew and chocolate milk gave no water; nethercoal did not fuel LSO's heater. (3) FC's other warm blocks, loose cobblestone and loose stone brick with lava in their cracks (`GetIsBlockWarm`, Server FCBlockLavaReceiver.java:99-109), and the post-1.5.2 candles, candle cakes and lantern gave no warmth. (4) Under Cold Hunger, LSO's overlay `legendarysurvivaloverhaul:cold_hunger` drew a second, vanilla-scale food bar below BTW's and added 10 to `rightHeight`, so BTW's pips rose to H-49. (5) BTW fat did nothing against cold. (6) LSO's fern crops on FC grass and sand took 1.20.1 bone meal: the item was used up and the plant grew |
| **Pattern** | **D** (FC-extend): BTW's heat, clothing, cooking, fuel and fat feed LSO's own data frameworks, and BTW's heal threshold gates LSO's regeneration step. Rebalance: none to LSO; every BTW value sits on LSO's own scale (torch 1.5, fire 7, lit furnace 6, campfire 10, magma block 12, lava 12.5; torch item 1.0; coal 600 heater ticks; milk 5 water) |
| **Implementation** | [`LsoClimateDataProvider`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/data/LsoClimateDataProvider.java) (runData provider `BTW LSO climate data`) writes LSO JSON from [`BtwThermalTable`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/thermal/BtwThermalTable.java) (`heatSources`, `vanillaHeatSources`, `clothing`, `hotMeals`, `vanillaHotMeals`, `fuels`), keyed through `FcRefs`, plus the manifest `data/betterthanwolves/compat_refs/lso.json` (14 block and 25 item fields) for BridgeSelfAudit's compat-refs audit; its `plan()` is shared with the start-up audit, and a heat tier it has no value for fails runData. [`LsoCompat`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/lso/LsoCompat.java), the only class that names an LSO class, is reached from `BTWCompat` (BTWCompat.java:142-150) and registers [`LsoFatInsulation`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/lso/LsoFatInsulation.java) (`PlayerTickEvent`, NORMAL), its own `ServerStartedEvent` audit (LOWEST) and, on the client dist only, [`LsoHudCompat`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/lso/LsoHudCompat.java) (a `FoodHudExtension` tint and a `RenderGuiOverlayEvent.Pre` cancel at HIGH). [`LsoRegenGateMixin`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/mixin/compat/lso/LsoRegenGateMixin.java) (`@Pseudo`, HEAD, cancellable, `require = 0`, `remap = false`) on `BodyDamageCapability.applyCustomHealthRegeneration(Player)` asks [`LsoRegenGate`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/lso/LsoRegenGate.java)`.blocks`, which lives outside the mixin package and fails open; the mixin also adds the marker `LsoRegenGate.Applied`, which LsoCompat checks. [`CompatMixinPlugin`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/CompatMixinPlugin.java) drops the mixin when LSO is absent. The lava-cracked rows are `HeatTier.LAVA_CRACKS` in BtwThermalTable (one row per odd meta, BtwThermalTable.java:91-94), shared with Cold Sweat (#030). The fern-crop rule is the general FC-ground relay of [`BoneMealPolicy`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/BoneMealPolicy.java)`.route(level, pos, state)` (BoneMealPolicy.java:133-141; #017, #026). Relies on #020 (foundation), #021 (hunger contract), #022 (FC actions) and #023 (HUD stack). Hooks: `BRIDGE_SPEC.md`, Mod compatibility (BTWCompat), BtwThermalTable and LsoRegenGate.Applied |
| **Effect** | **Heat**: unlit campfire embers (meta 8-11) 2; small, medium and large campfire 4 / 7 / 10; stoked fire 12; the lit hibachi's own body 1 (the fire it lights on top counts on its own); burning brick oven 6; burning finite and nether torches 1.5; BTW candle 1; smouldering log 3; burning ladder 7; loose cobblestone and loose stone brick with lava in the cracks (odd metas) 12. Unlit, burned-out, idle and lava-free states give nothing. The 17 candles and 17 candle cakes give 1 while lit and the lantern 1.5; `minecraft:torch` and `wall_torch` keep LSO's own files (also 1.5), and soul torches and soul lanterns are not BTW rows. **Clothing**: cold resistance wool 1.5 / 2.0 / 1.5 / 1.0 (head, chest, legs, feet; 6.0 a set), padded, tanned and gimp 1.0 / 1.5 / 1.0 / 0.5 (4.0); plate heat resistance 0.5 / 1.5 / 1.0 / 1.0 (4.0) and no cold resistance. **Limbs**: plate 0.15 and padded 0.05 on the limb of its slot. Held burning finite and nether torches warm like LSO's torch item (1.0). **Meals**: chicken and fish soup warm as food and drink (level 1, 1200 ticks) and give 4 water; hearty stew warms as food (level 2, 1800 ticks) and gives 3; mushroom stew, which FC cooks in the cauldron, gives 4 and keeps LSO's own warmth; every BTW drinkable (chocolate milk at default ids) gives 5, as LSO's milk. **Fuel**: each of BTW's 39 fuels runs LSO's heater `max(1, round(600 x FC burn time / 1600))` ticks, so one FC coal equals LSO's coal (600), nethercoal runs 1200 and the smallest fuels 9. **Fat**: FC fat 0-20 adds 0-2.0 cold resistance, linear, rounded to two decimals, as a transient ADDITION modifier (`e3a7185f-bdc6-37d1-ac73-276764715e52`, "BTW fat insulation") refreshed every 20 ticks on the server and never saved to NBT. **Regeneration**: under LSO's default config its custom regeneration (every 200 ticks, 6.0 exhaustion per HP, paid in FC hunger with FC's armour weight) runs only above 24/60 FC food; a blocked step neither heals nor costs hunger, and LSO tries again one interval later. With LSO's "Natural Regeneration Enabled" = true, LSO leaves the gamerule on and its custom regeneration off, and BTW's 30 s heal is the only clock. **HUD**: LSO's Cold Hunger bar is cancelled and BTW's pips turn frost blue (`0xFFB0E0FF`) while the `legendarysurvivaloverhaul:cold_hunger` effect lasts. **Bone meal**: on LSO's sun fern, ice fern and water plant crops standing on FC grass or sand, bone meal relays to the ground, which declines: the item is kept and the plant does not grow; their random growth and loot stay 1.20.1's. **Start-up**: `[BTW-LSO] data audit: <n> files expected, <m> loaded by LSO, <k> missing`, one WARN per missing file, and one line that names the regeneration clock and says whether the gate applied (WARNs when nothing heals, when the gate is missing, or when the gamerule disagrees with LSO's config) |
| **BTW adds** | BTW's progression is the answer to LSO's cold: a bigger fire, a stoked fire (BTW's hottest flame, just under lava), wool, padded and plate armour, cauldron soups, nethercoal in the heater and a well-fed body. LSO's thirst and regeneration pay in BTW's 60-point food and fat and keep BTW's 24/60 heal threshold, so AppleSkin's regeneration hint (#029) stays right with LSO. Cold Hunger reads on BTW's own food row |
| **Dual-sourced?** | Yes — LSO's systems, rates, costs and bundled data are untouched: BTW writes only files LSO does not ship (LSO's `minecraft` torch, wall-torch and mushroom-stew warmth files stay LSO's), and the gate only puts BTW's threshold in front of LSO's step. Without LSO the data is inert (LSO's data listeners are its only readers), the mixin is never prepared, and nothing is logged |
| **Data or code?** | Both: 126 generated JSON files at default ids (90 under `betterthanwolves`, 36 under `minecraft`) plus the manifest; three event handlers and one `@Pseudo` mixin (`remap = false`, no refmap entry) |
| **Alt rejected** | Switching LSO's overlapping systems off for BTW players (deactivating its thirst and wetness per player, telling admins to turn off its regeneration and costs) and shipping no data: it deletes LSO features instead of giving them BTW answers (Protocol #8). Gating regeneration through the `naturalRegeneration` gamerule: LSO gates its custom regeneration on its own config field (`Config$Baked`), not on the rule |
| **Alt rejected (2)** | Cancelling LSO's player tick to stop its regeneration, which would also stop its limbs and thirst. A permanent cold-resistance modifier through LSO's `TemperatureUtil`: it is saved to player NBT and could outlive the integration; the transient vanilla modifier is compared with the live one and re-applied within 20 ticks on a respawned or relogged player. Writing BTW files over LSO's own vanilla files: by pack order they would replace LSO's values |
| **Disables (justification)** | Under LSO's default config ("Natural Regeneration Enabled" = false) LSO sets `naturalRegeneration=false` at level load, which pauses BTW's free 30 s heal (#021), and LSO's limb-capped regeneration becomes the only clock, held to BTW's threshold above. That is LSO's own design for its health overhaul, and exactly one clock runs. A server that wants BTW's heal sets "Natural Regeneration Enabled" = true under `[core.misc]` in `config/legendarysurvivaloverhaul/legendarysurvivaloverhaul-common.toml`; LSO then leaves the gamerule on. Nothing else is disabled |
| **Known limitation** | Cold Sweat and LSO both read BTW's heat and fat, so with both installed heat counts twice; `BTWCompat` logs one WARN (BTWCompat.java:96-103) recommending "Temperature Enabled" = false in LSO, so Cold Sweat owns temperature and LSO keeps thirst and limbs (#030). Running `/gamerule naturalRegeneration true` while LSO's config is false runs both clocks until LSO rewrites the rule at the next level load; the start-up WARN sees only a mismatch present at server start. Bare-hand water drinking is not refused while food-poisoned: LSO's own raw water (3 water, a 75% chance of its Thirst effect for 300 ticks) plays that role, and gating it would need two injections into LSO internals (`DrinkBlockFluidMessage.DrinkWaterOnServer`, `CommonForgeEvents.onRightClickBlock`). `BreakEvent` fires once per FC destroy call, chew stages included, so LSO charges block-break thirst per hit on a chewed log (#022). Fuel and drinkable files have no FC field name, so only LsoCompat's audit, not BridgeSelfAudit, reports one whose BTW id moved. Which vanilla files LSO already ships is fixed from the 2.4.7 jar; a later LSO that ships candle or lantern data would compete with BTW's file by pack order. HarvestCraft drinks give no LSO water: LSO ships no `pamhc2` data and BTW writes none (#032). The gate target and the `Config$Baked` fields are LSO internals: a renamed method gives a WARN and an ungated LSO, while a changed method body would not be noticed. Out of scope, unchanged: BTW-native water purification (LSO's own blasting recipes purify water bottles and canteens, and the vanilla blast furnace recipe takes BTW's brick oven in the furnace's place under decision D-B, #019); FC damage sources as the damage types LSO's limb rules key on. LSO's sleep recovery, out of scope while BTW disabled beds, applies since 2026-10-21, when beds began to sleep (#037; not runtime-tested with LSO) |
| **Verified by** | Build (C2, 2026-10-17): `Mixin config OK (40 entries, all present, no unlisted @Mixin)` (41 since the hold-to-craft fix added `LivingEntityUseAccessor`) and `Jar bundling OK` (no LSO class or resource ships); runData's `BTW LSO climate data` writes the 126 files and the manifest. HEAD 3985147 baseline `lso_p0`: 19 gap checks fail and 13 controls pass (LSO heals 10 -> 11 at FC food 24 and 3; no BTW heat, clothing, limb, fuel, meal, water or fat data; LSO loads 21 temperature blocks, 39 items, 6 consumables, 8 fuel items, 24 thirst consumables and 4 body-part resistance items). After, dev server with LSO: `lso_p1` 86/86 — no heal and no cost at FC food 24 and 3, +1 HP for 6.0 exhaustion at 25 and for 12.0 in plate (armour weight 2.0); every heat tier with its meta and lit gates, lava-cracked stone 12, candle, candle cake and lantern, the torch still 1.5; in the world, none 11.2 < embers 13.0 < small 14.8 < medium 17.6 < large 20.3, and hibachi with fire 17.7 < hibachi with stoked fire 22.4 > large campfire; wool 6.0, padded, tanned and gimp 4.0, plate heat 4.0 and limbs 0.15, padded limb 0.05; held torches 1.0; hearty stew +3 water and hot food level 2 for 1800 ticks, chicken and fish soup +4 with warm food and drink for 1200, mushroom stew +4 with LSO's warmth, chocolate milk +5; nethercoal 1200 HEATING; fat 10 / 20 / 0 / 7.5 gives 1.0 / 2.0 / 0 / 0.75, absent on a new player object and back after one tick; the C1 harvest thirst (0.07) and the poisoned-drink refusal unchanged. Its log: `integration #031 enabled`, `[BTW-LSO] data audit: 126 files expected, 126 loaded by LSO, 0 missing`, `Mixing compat.lso.LsoRegenGateMixin` into `BodyDamageCapability`, the regeneration line (every 200 ticks, 6.0 exhaustion per HP, BTW food gate applied), `regeneration gate engaged` once, LSO's `Loaded 70 temperature blocks` (and 61 items, 9 consumables, 47 fuel items, 29 thirst consumables, 12 body part resistance items), compat-refs `43 entries, 0 stale`. `lso_p2` ("Natural Regeneration Enabled" = true) 4/4: the gamerule stays true, BTW heals above 24/60 and not at 24, and LSO's own clock heals nothing over 201 LSO ticks. `lso_p3` (no LSO) 3/3: no `[BTW-LSO]` line, no mixin line or class-load error, BTW's heal unchanged. `lso_p4` (with Cold Sweat) 3/3 and one WARN. `lso_p5` (`fcBlockCampfireLargeID=3000`) 3/3: compat-refs `1 stale (1 warned, 0 inert)`, `126 files expected, 124 loaded by LSO, 2 missing` (the moved campfire's heat and fuel files), the server starts and the medium campfire still reads 7. `gc_p6` 6/6: bone meal kept and age unchanged on a sun fern on FC grass (12 uses) and on a water plant on FC sand, and both still drop their seeds (HEAD: used up, and both grew). Client `lso_c1_hud`: under LSO's own Cold Hunger (FROSTBITE through `/temperature set`) the pips stay at `pipsTop=H-39` with `tint=FFB0E0FF` (HEAD: `H-49`, untinted); `hud_p2a` and AppleSkin with LSO (`as_p4`) pass. All six mods at once: no heal at FC food 3 and the large campfire at 10. Production: the reobf release jar on a Forge 47.3.0 dedicated server with all six mods logs `#031 enabled`, `Mixing compat.lso.LsoRegenGateMixin` into LSO's SRG jar, the gate applied, and `126 files expected, 126 loaded by LSO, 0 missing`. Not run: the ice fern (the same CropBlock rule) and a production client |
| **Net effect on FC loop** | Thirst, climate and limb damage join BTW survival, paid in BTW's 60-point hunger and fat. BTW's fires, wool, plate, cauldron soups and nethercoal answer LSO's cold, and BTW's heal threshold holds. Without LSO nothing changes |

### #032 — Pam's HarvestCraft 2

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Modern feature** | Pam's HarvestCraft 2 (HC) in four optional modules: `pamhc2crops` (97 crops and six wild gardens), `pamhc2trees` (50 fruit-tree saplings and 50 fruit blocks), `pamhc2foodcore` and `pamhc2foodextended` (meals, kitchen tools, HarvestCraft flour and dough); 1287 edible items in all. Its food values, its shortcut recipes (bread from raw grain, flour ground in the crafting grid, string from fibre crops), gardens that bone meal grows and spreads, and fruit that ripens on its own random tick. The addons `dtphc2` (Dynamic Trees, #027) and `sereneseasonsphc2crops` (Serene Seasons, #028) build on it |
| **Added** | Third-party mod for 1.20.1. Tested builds: pamhc2crops 1.0.3, pamhc2trees 1.0.2, pamhc2foodcore 1.0.5 and pamhc2foodextended 1.0.1, with dtphc2 1.20.1-1.3.2 and sereneseasonsphc2crops 1.20.1-1.0.0 (dev tokens `harvestcraft`, `dtphc2` and `ss-hc2crops`, Forge/build.gradle:1855-1873). `mods.toml` lists every module as optional, with BTW loading after it: Crops `[1.0.3,)`, Trees `[1.0.2,)`, Food Core and Food Extended `[1.0.0,)` (mods.toml:97-124) |
| **Decisions** | Compat-wave decisions (2026-10-11) #7 (modded fruit is kept, with FC values), #12 (HarvestCraft food tiers and raw-meat poisoning), #13 (the reroutes, including the `string_fiber` removal, and dung spreading gardens) and #18 (licences: no HarvestCraft class reference, nothing of HarvestCraft bundled, a player notice). C2 harvestcraft D1 (how the food values ship) and D2 (the dough economy), settled on 2026-10-17 by the maintainer's rulings: D1 (b), a general `tiers` rule in `btw_foods` carrying #12's values, instead of a generated per-item table (general rules over per-mod lists); D2 (b), HarvestCraft dough costs FC's three flour per dough (nearest FC relative: FC's own dough, Server FCRecipes.java:1426-1430). Ground meats take the values of their FC raw and cooked meat, which refines #12's flat 3 by the same rule. The standing rulings apply: bone meal is 4.B fertilizer (#017), post-1.5.2 plants grow at 4.B speeds by their lore (#018), integrate, don't disable (Protocol #8). One question is open (below) |
| **Conflict** | At 3985147, C1's general rules (#024-#026) already planted, grew, harvested and fertilized HarvestCraft crops and saplings the BTW way; the rest of HarvestCraft undercut BTW. Food: HC foods fed at their 1.20.1 nutrition times three (1287 items): a tomato gave 9 points of BTW's 60-point hunger bar and corned beef breakfast 132, more than the whole bar; raw ground meat poisoned only through #025's cold-crafted mark (it is crafted from raw beef or pork), and meals made from it never poisoned; HC seeds, grains and vegetables were not FC fodder. Recipes: three barley or three wheat made bread cold, bakeware and HC dough made bread in the grid, one flour made two HC dough, the grinder turned any flour plant into HC flour in the grid, two cotton made string, nothing of HarvestCraft's entered the millstone, and the stays-removed rule (#019) deleted the cattail torch recipe, disabling it rather than integrating it; at the FC workbench 21 Food Extended recipes returned another HarvestCraft recipe's item. Plants: bone meal grew and spread gardens, on grass and on sand, and ripened fruit; HarvestCraft fruit grows one age step on 1 in 5 random ticks at light 9 or more, the 1.20.1 sweet-berry rate, posts no growth event and grew in the End |
| **Pattern** | **D** (FC-extend): HarvestCraft content runs on BTW's soil, growth, machines and hunger, through general rules and data; **B** (gate): its shortcut recipes go through the millstone, BTW bread dough and the BTW finite torch; **C** (rebalance): its foods take FC values, capped at FC's hearty stew (10, Server FCItemFood.java:93). Nothing is removed except the one recipe the millstone replaces (see Disables) |
| **Implementation** | General rules from C1, unchanged: #024 (`btw_foods`, foods stack to 16), #025 (replacements by recipe id, tag-driven millstone recipes, the cooking import, raw-crafted marks, the FC workbench fallback), #026 (`FcSoil`, `ModdedPlantRules`, the immature-crop loot modifier, the plant stand-in, modded saplings as FC sapling twins). Added in C2 as general rules any data pack can use: the `tiers` field of [`BtwFoodTable`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/food/BtwFoodTable.java) (a band rule per mod namespace, BtwFoodTable.java:208-239, 343-379); [`DungSpreading`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/farming/DungSpreading.java) (`#betterthanwolves:dung_spreads`); the `#betterthanwolves:paced_like_sweet_berries` pace in [`TwinRegistry`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/TwinRegistry.java) (TwinRegistry.java:165-173, 413-429), which `TickPolicy` re-decides after every tag bind (TickPolicy.java:145-149); the FC-ground relay of [`BoneMealPolicy`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/BoneMealPolicy.java)`.route(BlockGetter, BlockPos, BlockState)` (BoneMealPolicy.java:124-154), shared with #031. HarvestCraft only: [`HarvestCraftCompat`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/compat/harvestcraft/HarvestCraftCompat.java) (the tooltip notice on every dist and the start-up summary, reached only through `BTWCompat`'s guarded call, BTWCompat.java:151-159) and [`HarvestCraftDataProvider`](Forge/src/main/java/btw/forge/data/HarvestCraftDataProvider.java) (DataGenerators.java:42-43), which writes 18 files under `Forge/src/generated/resources/data/` in every runData: BTW flour in `#forge:flour/flour`; the block tags `fc_soil_plants` and `dung_spreads` (the six gardens) and `paced_like_sweet_berries` (95 ids: the 50 HarvestCraft fruit, Dynamic Trees' apple and cocoa, dtphc2's 43 fruit and pods); two millstone rules; six recipe reroutes; five food files; the `compat_refs/harvestcraft.json` manifest for the moved-ID audit (#020). Formats and tags: `BRIDGE_SPEC.md`, Food values (BtwFoodTable, FcFoodValues), Recipe bridge (RecipeBridge, WorkbenchFallback) and Modded plants (FcSoil, ModdedPlantRules) |
| **Effect** | **Food**: every edible HarvestCraft item takes FC values from the table. Crops and Trees produce: hunger 1 at nutrition 4 or less, else 2, no fat. Food Core and Food Extended: 2 / 3 / 5 / 8 / 10 at nutrition 4 / 8 / 16 / 25 / above, fat 0.25 above the first band. FC feeds three times the hunger value, so a tomato gives 3 points, a stew 15 and the largest meals 30. Raw ground meats are their FC raw meat with FC's standard food poisoning (chance 0.3, 60 s): beef and pork 4, chicken, fish, mutton and rabbit 3, fat 0.25 (Server FCItemFood.java:23-24, 125-147; rabbit, which 4.B lacks, takes the lower value). Cooked ground meats are their FC cooked meat: beef and pork 5, the others 4. The entries use the six `#forge:groundmeats/<meat>` tags, because HarvestCraft also puts firm tofu in `#forge:groundmeats`; tofu stays safe. A meal whose recipe accepts raw ground meat is marked raw-crafted (#025) and can poison. HC seeds feed FC chickens, `#forge:grain` FC cows and sheep, `#forge:vegetables` FC pigs, at FC's base values. **Recipes**: three milled flour and a water bucket make BTW bread dough (`bread_grain`, FC's own dough recipe); bakeware and HC dough make BTW bread dough, which bakes the BTW way (`bread_dough`); HC dough costs three flour (`doughitem_x2`: mixing bowl, three `#forge:flour`, water and salt make one dough, where one flour made two); the grinder makes HC flour from BTW flour, one for one (`flour_x1_flour_plants`); cooking oil and a cattail make an unlit BTW finite torch (`torch_cattail`); `string_fiber` is removed. HarvestCraft recipes that take `#forge:flour` accept milled BTW flour. The millstone grinds every `#forge:flour_plants` item to 1 BTW flour and every `#forge:fiber` item to 2 hemp fibres; FC-mapped members (wheat, potato, apple) are skipped, so wheat keeps FC's own recipe; 40 millstone recipes with all four modules. **Plants**: crops and saplings follow #026. Gardens are FC wild vegetation for bone meal: on FC ground, and on modern soils that take wild plants, the bone meal relays to the ground, which declines, so the item is kept and nothing grows or spreads. BTW dung spreads gardens, through the garden's own bone-meal spread: main hand only, not for spectators, one dung per use (none in creative), and only where bone meal could be used (another mod's use-item DENY and the adventure-mode rule still apply). HarvestCraft fruit runs its own random tick on a quarter of random ticks, FC cocoa's 1 in 20 per stage (Server FCBlockCocoa.java:33-58), never in the End, and bone meal on it is kept and does nothing, as on FC cocoa. **Notice**: a dark-grey tooltip line, "Better Than Wolves: grown, eaten and cooked by BTW rules" (en_us.json:90), on every governed HarvestCraft item (foods, crop, sapling and garden items, flour plants, fibres, seeds, HC flour), not on kitchen tools. One INFO line at server start reports what the data changed |
| **Lore mapping** | HarvestCraft crops are domesticated crops and its fruit-tree saplings are saplings (#026). A garden is wild vegetation, which 4.B fertilizes only through its ground (FCBlockPlants), so bone meal does nothing to it; BTW dung spreads it instead (decision #13). Fruit ripening on a tree is cocoa. Grain and flour plants are wheat, and fibre crops are hemp: both go through the millstone. HC dough is FC dough, three flour each. A cattail dipped in oil is a finite torch. Ground meat is the meat it was ground from |
| **BTW adds** | 97 crops and 50 fruit trees join BTW's farming and forestry; 34 more flour plants and 6 fibre crops feed the millstone; BTW's flour, dough, oven and finite torch become the base of HarvestCraft's kitchen, and its 127 recipes that take HC dough now cost 4.B flour; dung gains a new use: it spreads wild gardens; 1287 foods count for BTW's hunger, fat, poisoning, stack size and animal fodder. The tier rule, `fc_soil_plants`, the dung tag and the fruit-pace tag are general: a data pack can apply them to any mod |
| **Dual-sourced?** | Yes — every HarvestCraft block, item, worldgen feature and loot table stays. Its recipe ids stay, except `string_fiber`: five recipes are rerouted in place and one is removed, by id, before the stays-removed rule (RecipeBridge.java:134, 140); every other HarvestCraft recipe is unchanged. Without HarvestCraft the data is inert (Forge conditions, `required:false` tag entries, millstone rules over empty tags): plain BTW logs `[BTW-Food] loaded 18 entries (0 tag, 0 tiered), 0 unknown ids skipped`, `Recipe replacements: 0 replaced, 0 removed, 6 skipped (6 file(s))` and `Tag machine recipes: 0 millstone recipe(s) added from 2 file(s)`. A data pack overrides any of the files by its path |
| **Data or code?** | Mostly data: the 18 generated files. Code: `HarvestCraftCompat` and the general rules above; no mixin. No HarvestCraft type is named anywhere and no HarvestCraft jar is a compile dependency: the content is identified by vanilla block classes, registry ids, tags and JSON (Pam's licence forbids redistribution and decompiling for non-educational use, Forge/build.gradle:1930-1932; the bundling guard admits only BTW's own classes, :1717-1728). The facts about HarvestCraft's own code in this entry (its fruit growth rate, its garden spread, its food values) were read with javap for interoperability; no HarvestCraft code is copied or shipped. Each `HarvestCraftCompat` handler catches `RuntimeException` and `LinkageError`, logs one WARN and switches itself off; runData fails if an FC object the data names does not resolve (HarvestCraftDataProvider.java:195-209) |
| **Alt rejected** | Mixins into HarvestCraft's crop, fruit and food classes, or code against its types: Protocol #4 and Pam's licence. Replacing HarvestCraft crops with FC crops: Protocol #5, and it would break `sereneseasonsphc2crops` and `dtphc2`. Adding BTW wheat to `#forge:grain` or `#forge:flour_plants`, or BTW raw meat to HarvestCraft's meat tags: HarvestCraft recipes would skip the millstone and the fire. Poisoning through `#forge:groundmeats`: it holds firm tofu. Overriding HarvestCraft recipes by data-pack order: not deterministic; replacements apply by id |
| **Alt rejected (2)** | A generated per-item food table (D1 (a)): Forge's datagen deletes the files of every provider that did not run (`HashCache.purgeStaleAndWrite`), so every runData would need HarvestCraft loaded, and Food Core builds differ (1.0.0 has 147 items, 1.0.3 and 1.0.5 have 181), so a fixed table misses meals on other builds; the tier rule gives the same values to whatever items the installed build has. For the dough: rerouting HC dough to BTW dough one for one and leaving one flour to make two dough (D2 (a): bread at a sixth of 4.B's flour), or making `bread_dough` take six HC dough (D2 (c): gates BTW bread only, while HarvestCraft's own dough meals stay cheap) |
| **Disables (justification)** | Bone meal no longer grows HarvestCraft crops, saplings, fruit or gardens: the 4.B ruling (#017); dung takes over the gardens. The `string_fiber` recipe (two fibres to string) is removed because fibre crops now mill into BTW hemp fibres (decision #13). Meals are capped at 10, FC's hearty stew, because one uncapped meal fills BTW's whole bar (decision #12). Every other shortcut is rerouted, not removed |
| **Known limitation** | HarvestCraft's own data bugs stay; they are HarvestCraft's to fix: Food Core 1.0.5 fails to parse three recipes (`melonpieitem`, `honeymuffinitem`, `caramelcupcakeitem_x4`); Crops 1.0.3 lists three global loot modifiers (`fern_drops`, `grass_drops`, `tall_grass_drops`) whose files it does not ship, so Forge cannot decode them; cooked ground pork has no recipe, so no BTW machine cooks it either. At the FC workbench 11 Food Extended recipes still return an earlier-id HarvestCraft recipe with the same grid (blackberry pie, carrot juice, chocolate milk, choco voxels, fig pie, lemon jelly, peach pie, popcorn, powdered donut, pralines, spring salad); vanilla also resolves these duplicates arbitrarily (`RecipeManager.getRecipeFor` takes the first match from a HashMap). The fruit pace is per stage, as for the sweet berry bush (#018): HarvestCraft fruit has 7 stages to FC cocoa's 2, so it ripens in about 3.5 times cocoa's time, and HarvestCraft's own check for the leaves above runs on the same paced tick, so fruit left without leaves drops up to about 4 times later. By code reading, a garden on red sand (a modern soil with no FC identity and no wild-plant hook) keeps 1.20.1 bone meal, as any plant on a modern soil does; not runtime-tested. The fibre yield (2 hemp fibres per HarvestCraft fibre item, against 4 per milled hemp, Server FCRecipes.java:4762) is a judgement call and one JSON number. The start-up summary reports four of the five reroutes and the `string_fiber` removal; `doughitem_x2` keeps its result item, so only the recipe proofs show it. No HarvestCraft food warms the player in Cold Sweat (#030) or LSO (#031), and HarvestCraft drinks give no LSO hydration (LSO ships no HarvestCraft data). `DungSpreading` runs a tagged block's own bone-meal code; a block whose code throws is left alone for the rest of the session. Food Extended reports version `0.0NONE` in dev and on a production server (its mods.toml takes `${file.jarVersion}`, and FML finds no version for this jar, although its manifest says 1.0.1); FML skips the range check for it, so BTW's `[1.0.0,)` bound on Food Extended is not enforced. Food Core 1.0.5 and Food Extended 1.0.1 are labelled for 1.20-1.20.4; they boot on Forge 47.3.0 in dev and on a production server. Not runtime-tested: the notice on a real client screen |
| **Open question (maintainer)** | Not decided: what an HC seed is worth. HarvestCraft gives its 97 seeds its shared food `Item.Properties` (and, per the after-run record, 13 more Crops items and 4 Trees spices), so they are edible, and the Crops tier values them like any produce of nutrition 4 or less: hunger 1, 3 points (proof D1b pins 3; at 3985147 the times-three conversion valued a seed at 9). 4.B has one edible seed, pumpkin seeds, an FCItemSeedFood of hunger 1, and FC never multiplies a seed food by three: 1 point (vanilla/server Item.java:288; Common FCItemSeedFood.java:89-94; carrots and potatoes, FC's other seed foods, restore 3, Item.java:367-368). Keep the tier's 3 points, or value HC seeds by 4.B's seed rule: the maintainer's call. Either way the seeds stay FC chicken fodder |
| **Verified by** | Dev-server proofs with `-PbtwDevMods=harvestcraft` (C2 after-runs, 2026-10-17/18). Every gap check fails on 3985147 (`hc_p2`'s D1b and D1c by recount of recorded values) and every check passes after C2. `hc_p0` without HarvestCraft 4/4 (no HarvestCraft recipe, FC wheat still mills to FC flour, FC's dough recipe unchanged; the plain-BTW lines above and `[MODDED-PLANTS] crops=0 saplings=0 modded-saplings-on-FC-rule=0 fc_soil_plants=0`). `hc_p1` recipes 34/34 (23 gap), in a fresh world and in `hc_p0`'s world, first made without HarvestCraft, and again after `/reload`: raw barley and wheat make no bread; three flour and water make BTW dough; one flour makes no dough and three make exactly one; the grinder takes only BTW flour; the cattail torch is a finite torch; cotton makes no string and mills to 2 hemp fibres; barley and acorns mill to BTW flour; wheat keeps FC's recipe and the potato gains none; the FC brick oven and campfire cook HC ground beef. The FC workbench sweep crafts every Food Core (178) and Crops (107) recipe and 858 of 869 Food Extended recipes; the other 11 return HarvestCraft's duplicates (21 at 3985147: the flour reroute freed the nine nut butters and the cornmeal). `hc_p2` food 38/38 (30 gap): tomato 9 -> 3, toast 15 -> 9, stew 45 -> 15, chicken dinner 63 -> 24, corned beef breakfast 132 -> 30 with no fat; raw ground beef 12 with poison (55 of 200 eats poisoned), raw ground fish 6 -> 9, cooked ground beef 24 -> 15 and safe; a gourmet beef patty made cold from raw ground beef is raw-crafted (59 of 200 eats poisoned); firm tofu never poisonous; HarvestCraft foods stack to 16; seeds, grain and vegetables feed FC chickens, cows and sheep, and pigs; the notice on a food, a seed and a garden, not on bread or a pot. `hc_p3` gardens and fruit 14/14 (10 gap): bone meal kept on gardens on grass and on sand, with no spread (at 3985147 it was used up and the gardens spread); 16 dung grew one windy garden into 7 (1 at 3985147); bone meal kept on fruit, fruit unchanged; no fruit growth in the End (age sum 0 of 8 fruit, 11 at 3985147); a 200-tick race at randomTickSpeed 300: 16 fruit summed 8 age steps (60 at 3985147; expected about 12 paced, 47 unpaced) beside 13 for 16 sweet berry bushes. `hc_p4` crops and saplings 17/17 before and after (the #026 rules, first run with HarvestCraft here). With Serene Seasons and `sereneseasonsphc2crops`: `hc_p5` 5/5 and the seasons run 7/7; with Dynamic Trees and `dtphc2`: `hc_p5` 5/5 and `dt_p8` 12/12 (#027).
